# Never-executed library code

Library functions that no vitest test executes, from one run of the full library suite (`npx vitest run` in packages/usd) with V8 precise coverage, generated by the tooling kept outside this repository at d7520616 (2026-11-12). That run: 876 test files, 9229 tests passed, 38 failed (known gaps), 0 skipped or todo.

Method. Every `pxr/**/src/**/*.ts` file is parsed with the TypeScript compiler API. Function declarations and expressions, arrow functions, methods, constructors and get/set accessors that have a body each count as one function; closures and accessors are counted as functions in their own right, the way llvm-cov counts them, so a method that ran can still list a closure inside it that never ran. A function counts as executed if any test file ran it at least once. Executed V8 functions are mapped back to the .ts source through the source maps vitest produces (a custom vitest coverage provider in the tooling kept outside this repository); no coverage package is used.

What this does not measure. The imaging tests that need a GPU run only in Safari through the storm-image harnesses (tests/browser/storm-image/**: testHdStDepthBias, testHdStBasicDrawing, testHdStSimpleLighting, testHdStShadersLighting, the texture registry tests), which are not instrumented. Imaging coverage, hgiWebGPU and hdSt in particular, is therefore understated. Imaging files that a Safari harness imports (statically, transitively) are marked **S** in the tables below: some of their never-executed functions may run there, but which ones is not measured. Importing a module does not mean its functions run. Coverage also says nothing about whether executed code was checked against upstream; it only shows where no test reaches at all.

Regenerated after test or library changes by the tooling kept outside this repository (FIXTURES.md); it runs the full suite once, about two minutes on an M-series Mac.

## Next tests to port

Upstream tests that would execute the largest never-executed ported areas, chosen by reading the upstream test sources against the never-executed function lists below. Every name is a registration in the OpenUSD 26.11.1 CMakeLists (cited) and is checked against pxr/testing/upstreamTestManifest.json each time this report is generated; none of them has a vitest port yet. Items are ordered by how many functions in the area are never executed. Tests marked GPU construct HdSt_TestDriver or an Hgi device upstream, so their ports belong in the Safari storm-image harnesses and will not move the numbers here until those harnesses are measured.

Areas left out because no unported upstream test reaches them: usdLux, usdVol, usdPhysics, usdRi and usdRender generated schema accessors (all registered tests of those libraries are ported; upstream reaches most accessors only from image tests such as testUsdImagingGLUsdLux), the Hd typed schemas for cameras, render settings, render products and back plates (no registered upstream test calls their typed accessors), the Sdf spec API (every registered Sdf test is ported; upstream drives SdfPrimSpec ordering and metadata through Usd and Python properties), hgiWebGPU (WebGPU only; exercised in Safari), and Trace, Tf diagnostics, UsdShade and the HdSt GL code generation (every registration with a TypeScript counterpart is now ported; the remaining Tf and Trace registrations are Python, C++ template, threading or performance tests). The Safari ports of testHdStBasicDrawing, testHdStTextureObjectRegistry, testHdStTextureHandleRegistry, testHdStSamplerObjectRegistry and testHdStDynamicUvTexture exist under pxr/imaging/hdSt/tests/upstream/*.ts but are not .test.ts files, so the manifest does not count them and their coverage of hdSt texture code is not measured here.

1. **Hdx tasks, cameras and lights**: 287 of 713 functions never executed. Port `testHdxCameraAndLight` (pxr/imaging/hdx/CMakeLists.txt:269), `testHdxRenderTask` (pxr/imaging/hdx/CMakeLists.txt:346). GPU (HdSt render delegate). testHdxCameraAndLight checks camera and light dirty-bit propagation through performance counters rather than pixels.
2. **Hd scene delegate, render index and change tracking (hd)**: 150 of 509 functions never executed. Port `testHdStRenderPass` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2891), `testHdStDrawItemsCache` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2472), `testHdStPrimGather` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2845), `testHdStInitRepr` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2632). GPU. They populate HdUnitTestDelegate and sync, collect and re-dirty rprims through HdRenderIndex and HdChangeTracker, the paths the scene-index tests do not reach.
3. **HdSt draw batching, dispatch buffers and culling**: 59 of 168 functions never executed. Port `testHdStDrawBatching` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2454), `testHdStIndirectDrawBatchCulling` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2625), `testHdStFrustumCullingCPU` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2547). GPU. testHdStDrawBatching compares batch membership to a stdout baseline; the culling tests exercise the dispatch buffer layouts.
4. **HdSt mesh, topology and primvar sync**: 28 of 181 functions never executed. Port `testHdStSubdivisionOsd3` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2909; partly ported), `testHdStMultipleFvarTopologies` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2829), `testHdStPrimvars` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2874). GPU. Refined (OpenSubdiv) topology, face-varying channels and primvar reallocation; the ported testHdStMeshTopology covers only the unrefined paths.
5. **Hio image reading and writing**: 8 of 42 functions never executed. Port `testHioImage` (pxr/imaging/hio/CMakeLists.txt:60). No GPU. Reads, writes and converts images through HioImage, including the stb paths that the texture tests do not reach.
6. **HdSt instancing**: 1 of 18 functions never executed. Port `testHdStInstancing` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2646), `testHdStInstancingUnbalanced` (pxr/imaging/hdSt/CMakeLists.txt:2731). GPU. Nested instancer primvar aggregation and instance index buffers.
